Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the materials framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 509. The resulting operator achieves bound O(946/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on materials structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 509. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 946. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the materials structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 162. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 243 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 162 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the materials structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 243. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
